## 2024-07-28 — Designing Games and Societies for Collective Flourishing

full file: `12-claude-ai-chats/2024-07-28_Designing_Games_and_Societies_for_Collective_Flourishing_7e0b6165.md`

翻译成中文：
As we contemplate the intricate tapestry of human interaction, we are compelled to consider: How do the structures we create shape the very essence of our collective journey?
The design of our games, be they literal or metaphorical, reflects and reinforces the underlying patterns of our society. When we reward the victors with tools that further cement their dominance, we inadvertently craft a narrative of inevitability—a tale where initial success begets an inexorable march towards total supremacy.
This phenomenon, which we might term "convergent dominance," is not merely a quirk of game design but a fundamental pattern that emerges in complex adaptive systems. It manifests in the accumulation of wealth, the consolidation of power, and the stratification of opportunity. The seeds of this pattern lie in the positive feedback loops that amplify initial advantages, creating what game theorists call a "Matthew effect"—where the rich indeed get richer.
Consider the paradigm of iterative games, where each round's outcome influences the next. In such systems, early leads can compound, transforming minor edges into insurmountable barriers. This dynamic is akin to the concept of "path dependence" in systems thinking, where historical contingencies shape future possibilities, often limiting the space of potential outcomes.
One might argue that this reflects a natural order—that those who excel should reap ever-greater rewards. Yet, this perspective fails to account for the broader implications on systemic resilience and collective flourishing. A system that converges too rapidly towards a singular dominant state loses the diversity and dynamism that drive innovation and adaptation.
To illustrate, let us consider a forest ecosystem. If a single species were to gain an overwhelming advantage—perhaps through an abundance of a particular nutrient—it might come to dominate the landscape. Yet, this monoculture would be fragile, vulnerable to shocks that a more diverse ecosystem could withstand. Our games, and by extension our societies, risk a similar fragility when we allow unchecked convergence.
The challenge, then, is to design systems that honor excellence while preserving the conditions for widespread participation and potential. This is not a call for artificial equalization, but rather for thoughtful mechanism design that recognizes the value of maintaining a vibrant, diverse field of engagement.
Imagine a game where victories unlock new modes of play rather than amplifying existing advantages. Or a society where success in one domain opens doors to explore others, rather than merely reinforcing established positions. Such structures could foster a rich ecology of achievement, where multiple paths to fulfillment coexist and interact.
At its core, this principle speaks to our deepest aspirations as a species. We are beings capable of remarkable individual achievement, yet our greatest triumphs have always been collective. Our shared journey is not a zero-sum game, but a collaborative expedition into the realms of possibility.
As we craft the rules that govern our interactions—in games, economies, and societies—we must ask ourselves: Are we creating systems that channel the river of human potential into a narrow, rushing stream? Or are we nurturing a delta of diverse tributaries, each contributing to a richer, more resilient whole?
In the end, the true measure of our games—and our civilizations—lies not in the heights achieved by a select few, but in the breadth and depth of flourishing they enable for all. Let us design with wisdom, compassion, and foresight, crafting spaces where victory serves not to end the game, but to expand its horizons for all who play.

Wether you believe in the invisible hand, decentralization, small government, socialism, or more niche ideology of how the system we live in should be

You think if the world work like this, then your life, or the society will be better

For each of the belief, there is a whole paradigm surrounding it, and if you pick words from this paradigm, reasoning with them, you will always reach a conclusion that this is the way society should be and the reason society is not going well, or the way you expect it to be, is because it did not go more aggressively towards that direction, and if we can move the society that way a bit more, the world and your life will be better. And if we don’t go that way, we will suffer more, or be more fucked up

And when society starts to fail, your ideal does not get actualized, you look at all the bad things that happen, once again, deploy the paradigm of your own belief, to make sense of what is going on, and concludes that your society did not go to the right direction because of the people who see things differently from you, who come from their paradigm, think the society should go other directions, and acted on it, and fucked it up. And you say the other is evil, they believed in the wrong, or fucked up shit, and you tag them with one identity and proudly giving yourself another identity, you project all wrongness onto them and starts fighting with them, try to use all the means to eliminate their power so that once you become the one who is holding power, the world will runs your way and be redesigned with your paradigm, with your own ideas.

You starts to justify violent and demoralizing tactics, because winning them is more important than anything else.

You start to wanting to eliminating them, you don’t see them as another human who just believe in different things, you see them as obstacles

Why force everyone to play the same game?

Why not give each freedom, liberty, and their own way of exploring their own way of organizing society, to establish and play their own game?

So at least, if one game fail, you can join another one

Forcing everyone to live in a neo-liberal world, a socialist world, capitalist world, or and libertarian world, itself, is tyranny

从来以开放包容、从容幽默、优雅得体见称的美国，如今却突然变得如此狭隘、焦虑、敏感。竞选语汇变得如此不堪，政客争权夺利手段也如此下作。而从大选折射出的美国政治状况看，华盛顿权势集团内部的基本政治共识已不复存在，其“症状”似乎确证了政治学者弗朗西斯·福山此前对美国政治的系统诊断：统治集团日趋悬浮于社会诉求之外，不仅在利益上出现严重内卷，而且在话语和观念形态上与民众的日常感受发生严重脱节，建制政客沉醉于一整套梦幻般的“政治正确”语汇，下层民众在这套强大的权威体系中日趋感到无力且无助，整个社会弥漫着严重的受挫情绪以及因之而生的不满和怨怼;裙带主义日趋成为权势集团日常运转的基本潜规则，自由民主日趋寡头化;政客被财团势力裹挟，党争渐趋白热化，致使公共事务所必须的基本共识难以达成，导致美国政治陷入彼此掣肘、相互拆台的恶性循环;……思维僵化与权力僵局彼此颉颃，使得任何形式的改革举措变得举步惟艰，民主政治开始陷入了一种“功能失调性的政治均衡”（dysfunctional political equilibrium）。民主制（democracy）俨然沦落为“否决制”（vetocracy）

According to Fukuyama, one of the main critiques of *The End of History* was of his aggressive stance towards postmodernism. Postmodern philosophy had, in Fukuyama's opinion, undermined the ideology behind liberal democracy, leaving the western world in a potentially weaker position. The fact that Marxism and fascism had proven untenable for practical use while liberal democracy still thrived was reason enough to embrace the hopeful attitude of the Progressive era, as this hope for the future was what made a society worth struggling to maintain. Postmodernism, which, by this time, had become embedded in the cultural consciousness, offered no hope and nothing to sustain a necessary sense of community, instead relying only on lofty intellectual premises.

## 2024-08-23 — Tracing the Waves of Macro-Historical Thought

full file: `12-claude-ai-chats/2024-08-23_Tracing_the_Waves_of_Macro-Historical_Thought_7b90d1af.md`

give a lecture based on: In this session, we will review the basic concepts of macro-historians since the publication of The Decline of the West from Oswald Spengler. We will distinguish three major waves of macro-history: The first wave of culturalist (Toynbee, Quigley, Sorokin) and ‘spiritualist’ (De Chardin, Aurobindo) authors, which were published mostly in the post WWII period before the 60s The second wave of ‘neo-materialist’ (or neo-marxist) world-system analyst authors (Fernand Braudel, Immanuel Wallerstein), starting in the 60-70s A third wave of ‘Big History’ authors, which starts its narrative at the Big Bang, seeks common patterns of evolution and change based on systems theory, complexity science and cybernetics. The came of age in the 90s, replacing the hegemony of world-systems analysis in academia. We will focus on operative concepts such as ‘instruments of expansion’ (Quigley), ‘challenge and response’ (Toynbee); ‘ideate vs sensate’ civilizational models (Sorokin), etc .. The stress of this session will be on first wave authors.

In the face of global challenges, it may seem naive to focus on meaning and beauty. However, it is precisely in difficult times that we most need hope and a connection to what makes life worth living.
By reconnecting with our sources of meaning, articulating them clearly, and creating beautiful, inspiring ways to share them with others, we can build a movement driven by love for life rather than fear of death. This approach has the potential not only to transform our individual lives but to create a more vibrant, fulfilling world for all.

article:
### 加入706清迈社区，共创未来乌托邦！

**欢迎来到706清迈**——这是我们706社区在海外建立的首个共居空间，也是我们对co-living概念多年来探索与实践的精华结晶。在这个全球生产力即将被AI解放的历史节点上，我们正在共同探索一种全新的生存方式，拥抱技术，探寻后工作时代的生活意义与社会组织模式。

你是否曾梦想生活在一个不受传统束缚、自由追求个人梦想的社区？一个科技、艺术与哲学融合的地方？我们正在构建一个全新的梦想家社区，一个超越现实、面向未来的乌托邦，邀请你一同加入，成为这场集体创想的先驱者。

**这里，我们相信：**
* **每个人都有独特的梦想**，并且这些梦想值得被实现。
* **平静与幸福**可以通过减少外部的追求和恐惧，专注于内在成长来实现。
* **技术与艺术的交汇**能激发无限可能，我们拥抱分布式技术、去中心化的共识经济，支持每个人用自己的方式贡献并交换价值。
* **友谊是幸福的基石**，我们创建了一个以协作、共享和支持为核心的生活方式，在这里，你将与志同道合的人们一同生活、探索和创造。
* **面向未来的生活实验场**：我们不仅是一个共居社区，更是一个充满可能性的试验场，旨在探索AI、Web3等技术下，人类如何重新定义工作与生活的边界。
**我们为你提供：**
* **未来生活方式实验**：从AI自动化到Web3去中心化，我们将与社区成员一起，探索后工作时代的新生活模式，打造全新的社会协作和自我实现路径。
* **去中心化的经济体系**：探索代币化的价值交换系统，支持你用技能、知识和创造力获得回报，摆脱传统货币束缚。
* **集体智慧与创造力的支持**：跨学科的讨论、创作工作坊，涵盖哲学、科技、艺术等多领域，帮助你探索未知，启发心灵。
* **个人成长与深度友谊**：你将生活在一个重视友谊与合作的社区，彼此支持，共同成长。
**我们在寻找：**
* **探索者与梦想家**：如果你对AI、Web3、后工作时代的人类生活方式充满好奇，愿意亲身参与未来的实验与实践，那么706清迈就是你最理想的家园。
* **共创未来的行动者**：我们寻找不仅仅有想法，还愿意付诸行动的人。无论你是技术专家、艺术家、哲学家，还是梦想改变世界的普通人，我们都期待与你携手共创。
* **开放与协作精神**：你愿意在集体中分享、互助，共同探索未来的可能性，用多元的智慧推动共居社区的成长。
**立即申请，加入未来的创造者行列：**
如果你对我们的理念感到共鸣，想要了解更多关于如何加入梦想家社区，请点击下方链接或联系我们的团队。一起迈入这个融合了哲学、科技与艺术的全新世界，创造你理想中的未来！
**让我们一起，共筑梦想，赋予未来无限可能！**

**Why Chiangmai? **清迈作为全球数字游民的首选城市，吸引了来自世界各地的大量年轻人、艺术家、程序员和创新者，他们在这里共同追求自由与创造。清迈不仅拥有丰富的自然资源和宜人的气候，还提供了多样化的生活与娱乐选项，城市的活力和独特的文化氛围让人们在这里能够找到工作与生活的理想平衡。高速的互联网连接使得清迈成为远程工作和数字创新的理想场所，同时，这座城市也是华语区Web3发展的重要基地之一，聚集了众多新思想和人类组织方式的探索者和去中心化技术的先驱。在签证政策上，清迈以其全球最友好之一的居住政策，使得人们可以轻松在这里长期定居、探索和创造未来的生活方式。


---

## 2024-09-15 — The Systemic Challenges of Capitalism and Inequality

full file: `12-claude-ai-chats/2024-09-15_The_Systemic_Challenges_of_Capitalism_and_Inequality_c9581cc5.md`

translate my note:

社会是一个巨大的系统，尤其是当下的社会，在全球化的进程之下，所有人都被资本绑定在了一起，货物的流通，生存物资的分配，工作以及消费，都在这一套巨大的市场与资本逻辑之下进行。 正是因为这套系统的存在以及对于这个系统的运行的共识，我们才可以coordinate，以此维护我们全球社会的正常运转

在历史的长河中，伴随着生产关系与权利分配的变化和科学技术的发展，我们的社会也在一直不断的自我进化，一套社会规范的崩塌与另一个社会的建立，一次次的发生。 文明也在不断的交替，但今天我们与以往都不同的是，我们的文明收敛到了一个全球的文明，各个国家之间的关系随着全球化的进程被紧紧的绑定在了一起，如果我们当下的社会系统崩塌，则意味着所有humanity都会suffer，而与之对立的，如果我们可以共同创造一个健康的社会系统，让我们的文明更加的进步、自由、稳定与繁荣，世界上的所有人都会因此受益

不幸的是，我们看到，在过去的几十年里，社会的进程似乎在向相反的方向演进，虽然我们有了互联网，有了巨大的算力，有了更好的医疗技术，但是我们的社会却走向了更加极端的贫富差距，commons的力量，民主与自由也在不断被削弱，生态系统也在逐渐崩溃。我们似乎走向一个科幻小说中所描述的反乌托邦的cyberpunk世界，而非一个更加繁荣、人们生活的更加幸福、更加平等和自由的以人为本的社会。 

同时，传统的意义与道德也在世界快速变化的过程中逐渐解体，我们变得更加关注个体的利益而非利他，我们变得更加保守或者更加极端，民族主义和身份政治的抬头，不同阵营之间的对立在不断加剧。我们好似进入了一个更加混乱与不稳定的时代

我们或许可以叫它metacrisis，在历史上，从未有任何一个时代我们面临着如此多的问题的同时，又感到手足无措。但是我们又必须去寻找这一系列危机背后的原因，看到背后的underlying cause，我们才可以去做出改变，重新让我们的社会变得更加适宜人类，而非滑向一个反乌托邦，战争或者虚无的未来

如同之前提到的，我们的世界是一个巨大的system，而人作为在这套系统中决策的agent，我们所adopt的叙事，ideology和价值观，对于这个系统的演化和运转起着决定性的作用。我们在做出一个决定的时候，以何种目的出发，也决定了我们社会系统会向哪个方向演进。

无论是资本主义，封建主义、社会主义还是集权主义，这些社会系统在演化的过程中，都有它自己的内在规律，这些社会将什么样的个体筛选成权利的持有者，以及这些拥有权利的人如何去行使他们的权利，如何去分配社会资源，如何决定社会价值，在很大的程度上决定了这些系统的发展。同时我们也可以看到，资源与机会并非无限，我们已经reach到了我们的planet boundary，如果越来越少的人拥有越来越多的资源，对于更多的人来讲他们就会有更少的社会机遇与自由

以资本主义来说，当一个个体在决策的过程中可以更好的optimize资本使用率，他就会更容易在这个过程中获得更多的资本与权利，因此也会逐渐获得更多的对于社会系统的影响力以及话语权。这让我们的社会中出现了一个将资本的最优化者放到行使并且获得社会权利位置上，也同时给予了他们更多的话语权，从而定义社会中什么是成功，以及成为其他人学习的榜样，这会形成一个feedback loop，最终使得权利以及社会影响力向最擅长于优化资本的人倾斜，直到他们获得社会中绝大多数的权利，通过资本的方式

而这个过程并非筛选最好的人，最有道德以及最可以深刻思考和看到社会规律的决策者，它仅仅是在挑选最擅长优化资本的人，如果一个个体可以以牺牲道德来换取利益，他则可以更好的在这个系统中获得更多的权利，从而变得对这个社会更加有影响力。当这个社会中最有影响力的人，最具有话语权的人以及最可以影响社会走向的人变成了以资本优化逻辑为思考范式的个体的时候，我们的正义、公平、以人为本的社会就会变成一个以逐利为核心的社会，我们的价值观也会向优化资本，而非让人生活的更好倾斜，这会导致moloch的出现，我们为了去优化可以获得权利的value， throw other values under the bus

对于一个集权主义社会来讲，最长袖善舞，最擅长玩政治游戏、最擅长斗争的个体将会逐渐攀升到这个社会的权利顶端，他们以他们斗争的思维和对于权利追逐的思考范式决定着整个社会的走向，为了权利而throw other values under the bus，以及更进一步的去获得更多的权利

当优化者掌握资本，资本就可以为了获得更多的资本而不切手段，从而让整个社会的财富向上收敛，以及为了资本回报而加剧剥削和让世界金融化，这个过程会导致社会的贫富差距越拉越大，最终使得大多数人的生活境遇变得更差，而非更好


一个集权的社会的权利保有者也会因为去优化权利而将humanity作为次要concern，从而将社会向一个绝大多数人失去权利和赖以生存资源的方向推动

这些问题的背后的根本原因是moloch，这个社会选择了某一种value作为权利的象征，最擅长于去优化这些value的人，在竞争的过程中会逐渐获得权利，从而以他们的方式和思考来控制我们的社会系统，并且throw other values under the bus

而这种竞争本身也是残酷的，当你面对着最大化利益与道德或损坏他人的利益的选择中，你面对的选项就是去最求最大化的利益，或者去选择一个对humanity更好的，更加正义公平的选项，但是你同时也面对着其他优化者的竞争，并且你理解他们可能会为了利益而抛弃其他value，因此你或做出利益最大化的选择，或者将权利拱手相让的同时做出对humanity更好的选择，但是in the long run，你会被out compete， 而那些忽略其他的value和价值的竞争者依然会最终获得最多的权利，并且将社会的走向拉向他们的逻辑与选择的方向

因此对于当下的社会来说，这个underlying cause导致了大量的问题在面对竞争的情况下，尤其是竞争决定着权利分配的情况下，导致其他value被牺牲掉，导致自私自利成为最好的博弈选择的同时让社会系统变得更加畸形、更加不平等、更加无法应对系统性问题，以及为了个人权利的优化使得我们没有办法在更高的层面上，例如系统的层面上对社会更好的optimization，是我们应该面对和解决的首要社会问题

## 2024-09-17 — Translating Article on 706 Chiang Mai Co-Living Space

full file: `12-claude-ai-chats/2024-09-17_Translating_Article_on_706_Chiang_Mai_Co-Living_Space_8ce651d2.md`

翻译成中文：

[attachment paste.txt]

**"Introducing a New Kind of Space**

Life is about more than just work. It's about more than the daily grind, the paycheck, and the routines that slowly chip away at our passions and dreams.

What if I told you that there’s a place where none of that applies? A place where the very essence of what makes us curious, creative, and connected is not just preserved but amplified?

Today, we’re introducing something that’s not just a new kind of living space. No, it’s more than that. It’s a revolution in how we live, how we create, and how we connect. It’s a place designed for people who refuse to settle for the ordinary, for those who believe that life should be an exploration of ideas, a pursuit of passions, and a journey of constant growth.

This is **706 Chiang Mai**—a co-living space unlike any other.

---

**A Place for Dreamers and Doers**

In university, we had these communities, didn’t we? Student clubs. Small groups of people driven by a common purpose, a shared passion, or maybe just a wild curiosity. We came together to explore ideas, take risks, and sometimes, fail spectacularly. But through it all, we grew. We found meaning in those connections, in the shared purpose, in the ability to freely express ourselves and contribute to something larger.

That essence of connection and purpose—it’s what made those clubs so special. But after graduation, something changes. Life becomes more fragmented, more focused on the transactional, and less on the communal. We lose that spark of experimentation, the thrill of being in a space where our ideas matter.

Well, that changes now.

---

**706 Chiang Mai is a return to that spirit—only this time, it’s for life.**

Imagine a place where your ideas are not only heard, but where the entire community is built to help you bring them to life. A place where you can walk out of your room and instantly be surrounded by others who are just as curious, just as creative, and just as driven as you are. A place where your passion, your unique perspective, isn’t just welcomed—it’s essential.

This is a home for those who want to live on the edge of innovation, of art, of philosophy, of technology. A home for those who refuse to be defined by a job title or a nine-to-five. This is for the explorers, the thinkers, the creators. For those who know that the world is changing and want to be the ones shaping that change.

---

**A Space for Digital Nomads to Thrive**

We’re starting this movement in **Chiang Mai**, a place that has become a global hub for digital nomads—a community of explorers who’ve chosen freedom, mobility, and flexibility over the conventional. Digital nomads are the pioneers of the new world. They are the ones redefining what it means to work, to live, and to grow in a world that’s increasingly decentralized and connected.

And at 706 Chiang Mai, we’ve created a space where they can truly thrive.

Here, we understand the needs of digital nomads: the need for fast, reliable internet, for quiet spaces to focus, and for inspiring environments to create. But beyond that, we understand the deeper desires: the longing for connection, for community, and for a sense of belonging even while traveling the world.

This co-living space is designed specifically with digital nomads in mind. It’s a place where you can do your best work, while also having the freedom to explore new passions, engage in meaningful conversations, and form lifelong connections with like-minded individuals.

---

**A Community That Grows With You**

At 706 Chiang Mai, you’re not just a guest passing through. You’re part of something much bigger. This is a decentralized, self-organizing community where every voice matters, where every idea has a place, and where every resident contributes to the collective growth. 

Here, digital nomads can fulfill their professional needs while being part of a vibrant, curious, and collaborative environment. It’s not just a place to get your work done—it’s a place to create, to connect, and to push the boundaries of what you thought was possible.

Whether you’re launching a startup, writing a book, or simply taking time to recharge, **706 Chiang Mai** offers you the infrastructure to succeed, the community to inspire you, and the environment to help you thrive.

---

**The Future of Living**

At 706 Chiang Mai, we don’t just talk about the future. We live it. We’re creating a world where technology, creativity, and community come together to form something entirely new. This is a space where digital nomads, artists, technologists, philosophers, and entrepreneurs converge. A space where the lines between work, play, learning, and creating blur, because that’s where the magic happens.

We believe that in this future, life isn’t about clocking in and out. It’s about curiosity. It’s about experimentation. It’s about finding joy in creation and in the connections you make along the way.

---

**Why This Matters**

At the heart of this co-living space is a simple, yet profound belief: **that life is best lived together, in collaboration, in curiosity, and in constant growth.**

We’ve lost that in society. But here, in 706 Chiang Mai, we’re bringing it back. And not just for a few years like in university, but for a lifetime. This is a community that will grow with you, challenge you, and inspire you, just as you will inspire it.

You see, the world needs more places like this. More spaces where people can be more than their job titles. More places where ideas flourish, where creativity thrives, and where community takes precedence over competition.

**706 Chiang Mai is more than a place to live—it’s a place to belong.**

---

Welcome to your new home. Welcome to a place where you can be exactly who you are, while discovering all that you could be. Welcome to 706 Chiang Mai—a new kind of living for a new kind of world.

## 2024-10-23 — Evaluating Vitalik's Proposal for Ethereum Alignment

full file: `12-claude-ai-chats/2024-10-23_Evaluating_Vitalik_s_Proposal_for_Ethereum_Alignment_a5e8a348.md`

this is from vitalik's blog, can you come up with a few critical and deep questions that related to the feasibility of this idea? I'll choose the best ones to ask him tomorrow

[attachment paste.txt]
One of the most important social challenges in the Ethereum ecosystem is balancing - or, more accurately, integrating, decentralization and cooperation. The ecosystem's strength is that there is a wide array of people and organizations - client teams, researchers, layer 2 teams, application developers, local community groups - all building toward their own visions of what Ethereum can be. The primary challenge is making sure that all these projects are, collectively, building something that feels like one Ethereum ecosystem, and not 138 incompatible fiefdoms.

To solve this challenge, many people throughout the Ethereum ecosystem have brought up the concept of "Ethereum alignment". This can include values alignment (eg. be open source, minimize centralization, support public goods), technological alignment (eg. work with ecosystem-wide standards), and economic alignment (eg. use ETH as a token where possible). However, the concept has historically been poorly defined, and this creates risk of social layer capture: if alignment means having the right friends, then "alignment" as a concept has failed.

To solve this, I would argue that the concept of alignment should be made more legible, decomposed into specific properties, which can be represented by specific metrics. Each person's list will be different, and metrics will inevitably change over time. However, I think we already have some solid starting points.







Open source - this is valuable for two reasons: (i) code being inspectable to ensure security, and more importantly (ii) reducing the risk of proprietary lockin and enabling permissionless third-party improvements. Not every piece of every application needs to be fully open source, but core infrastructure components that the ecosystem depends on absolutely should be. The gold standard here is the FSF free software definition and OSI open source definition.
Open standards - striving for interoperability with the Ethereum ecosystem and building on open standards, both the ones that exist (eg. ERC-20, ERC-1271...) and those that are under development (eg. account abstraction, cross-L2 transfers, L1 and L2 light client proofs, upcoming address format standards). If you want to introduce a new feature that is not well-served by existing standards, write a new ERC in collaboration with others. Applications and wallets can be rated by which ERCs they are compatible with.
Decentralization and security - avoiding points of trust, minimizing censorship vulnerabilities, and minimizing centralized infrastructure dependency. The natural metrics are (i) the walkaway test: if your team and servers disappear tomorrow, will your application still be usable, and (ii) the insider attack test: if your team itself tries to attack the system, how much will break, and how much harm could you do? An important formalization is the L2beat rollup stages.
Positive-sum
Toward Ethereum - the project succeeding should benefit the whole Ethereum community (eg. ETH holders, Ethereum users), even if they are not part of the project's own ecosystem. Specific examples include using ETH as the token (and thus contributing to its network effect), contributions to open source technology, and commitments to donate a % of tokens or revenue to Ethereum ecosystem-wide public goods.
Toward the broader world - Ethereum is here to make the world a more free and open place, enable new forms of ownership and collaboration, and contribute positively to important challenges facing humanity. Does your project do this? Examples include applications that bring sustainable value to broader audiences (eg. financial inclusion), % donations to beyond-Ethereum public goods, and building technology with utility beyond crypto (eg. funding mechanisms, general computer security) that actually gets used in those contexts.




Ethereum node map, source ethernodes.org



Obviously, not all of the above is applicable to each project. The metrics that make sense for L2s, wallets, decentralized social media applications, etc, are all going to look very different. Different metrics may also change in priority: two years ago, rollups having "training wheels" was more okay because it was "early days"; today, we need to move to at least stage 1 ASAP. Today, the most legible metric for being positive sum is commitments to donate a percentage of tokens, which more and more projects are doing; tomorrow we can find metrics to make other aspects of positive-sumness legible too.

My ideal goal here is that we see more entities like L2beat emerging to track how well individual projects are meeting the above criteria, and other criteria that the community comes up with. Instead of competing to have the right friends, projects would compete to be as aligned as possible according to clearly understandable criteria. The Ethereum Foundation should remain one-step-removed from most of this: we fund L2beat, but we should not be L2beat. Making the next L2beat is itself a permissionless process.

This would also give the EF, and other organizations (and individuals) interested in supporting and engaging with the ecosystem while keeping their neutrality, a clearer route to determine which projects to support and use. Each organization and individual can make their own judgement about which criteria they care about the most, and choose projects in part based on which ones best fit those criteria. This makes it easier for both the EF and everyone else to become part of the incentive for projects to be more aligned.

You can only be a meritocracy if merit is defined; otherwise, you have a (likely exclusive and negative-sum) social game. Concerns about "who watches the watchers" are best addressed not by betting everything on an attempt to make sure everyone in positions of influence is an angel, but through time-worn techniques like separation of powers. "Dashboard organizations" like L2beat, block explorers, and other ecosystem monitors are an excellent example of such a principle working in the Ethereum ecosystem today. If we do more to make different aspects of alignment legible, while not centralizing in one single "watcher", we can make the concept much more effective, and fair and inclusive in the way that the Ethereum ecosystem strives to be.

宣言
*我们将在此次大理 Web3 街(gāi)会「瓦猫之夏」搭建出赋能、开放和协作的空间，将顶尖的技术人才、创业者、孵化器和项目方团队以及 DAO 的践行者聚集在一起，彼此深度对话、相互联系和共同创造。我们希望你可以在这里找到同行者，将会场变成共同前进的起点，促进跨社区交流，打破社区 / 团队间的边界，让技术灵感和对未来可能性的想象自由翱翔。同时，我们也会将大理社区的轻松氛围与 Web3 文化展示给所有的与会者。*

*我们创建了 瓦猫（Wamo）——大理Web3的吉祥物。它基于一个大理神话生物——瓦猫，一个非常强大的守护神。 它代表了积极的外部性、理性的体现、共识、开源和乐观的精神。*

## 2024-11-03 — The Dangers of Unchecked Power Accumulation

full file: `12-claude-ai-chats/2024-11-03_The_Dangers_of_Unchecked_Power_Accumulation_d72393cf.md`

1. The agent with more power capture efficiency will accumulate more power over time through compound advantages
2. The agent that ruthlessly optimizes for power alone, while ignoring all other values and externalities, will achieve the highest power capture efficiency
3. Through iterative competition cycles, agents with highest power capture efficiency eliminate or subsume other agents (who may hold more balanced or ethical values)
4. As these hyper-efficient agents rise to the top of the power hierarchy, they gain increasing discourse power and become models for others to emulate
5. These dominant agents, now in positions of systemic influence, actively reshape other agents' values toward pure power optimization
6. Other agents face a brutal choice: either adopt the same reductionist optimization or face extinction. Many are also unconsciously captured by the dominant value paradigm
7. The system enters a dominant self-reinforcing loop - as power concentrates, it accelerates power capture, which enables more power concentration. This loop intensifies with each cycle
8. As power concentrates at the top (Matthew effect), competition among remaining agents becomes increasingly fierce as they fight over diminishing resources. This intensified competition forces even more extreme optimization behaviors, further accelerating the primary loop
9. In pursuit of ever-higher power capture efficiency, dominant agents will sacrifice even the foundational values and rules that keep the system stable - corrupting and eroding the very mechanisms that enabled their rise
10. This intensifying loop drives the system to become increasingly reductionist, optimizing solely for power while growing increasingly blind to its own destabilizing effects, and actively destroying its own foundations
11. The intensity of value capture continues to increase until the system loses fundamental stability and collapses under its own optimization pressure

can you explain this better?
